DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player guides a constantly advancing dot that is pulled toward a gravity anchor on either side; tapping flips the anchor, curving the dot’s path to weave through a series of horizontally offset gaps.

## Player
The avatar is a small glowing dot. Its only verb is to *toggle* the gravity anchor left or right (a tap or click). This single action determines the curvature of its trajectory.

## Core loop
The dot moves forward automatically. The player watches upcoming obstacles and decides, on each beat, which side to pull the dot toward. Switching the anchor gently steers the dot into the next gap. After the dot passes a gap, a new offset gap appears, and the decision‑making rhythm repeats, keeping the player engaged in a continuous flow of anticipation and reaction.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Offset Gap Walls**: Solid vertical barriers with a single horizontal opening that the dot must align with. The opening’s position shifts left or right each time.
- **Moving Sweepers**: Horizontal bars that glide across the playfield, briefly blocking portions of a gap and demanding precise timing.
- **Closing Spikes**: Short spikes that emerge from the walls when the dot lingers too long, encouraging forward momentum.
Each hazard threatens collision and forces the player to plan anchor switches ahead of time.

## Score
Score increases each time the dot successfully passes through a gap. Bonus points are awarded for chaining consecutive flawless passages without a miss.

## Win and lose
The player loses instantly upon colliding with any solid part of an obstacle; a “Game Over” overlay appears with the final score and restart instructions. The player wins by navigating a predefined sequence of gaps (e.g., completing the final gap of a level) without collision, triggering a celebratory “You Win!” overlay.

## Feel
A rhythmic, flowing experience with a neon‑glow aesthetic against a dark backdrop. The pace feels steady yet increasingly tense as gaps shift, and subtle synthetic tones punctuate each successful maneuver and collision.

## Why it's fun
Each tap instantly reshapes the dot’s path, turning simple timing into a satisfying dance of weaving through tight gaps.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json — create index.html with required header, canvas, restart button and matching meta.json.
2. Game state objects — declare player, hazards, score, and phase/state objects at top of script with initial values.
3. Animation loop — implement startLoop with cancelAnimationFrame, requestAnimationFrame loop, try/catch, and canvas clearing.
4. Input handling — set up keys Set, keydown/keyup listeners, R-key and restart button to call reset().
5. Player movement — apply dt-based physics, anchor toggle effect, velocity caps, and position updates each frame.
6. Hazard system — define hazard data structures, spawn rules with SAFE_RADIUS, and dt-scaled motion.
7. Collision detection — check player vs hazards, trigger gameover phase and stop gameplay loop.
8. Scoring — increase score on successful gap passes, draw score on canvas top-left.
9. Rendering — draw background, hazards, player dot (high contrast), and HUD each frame.
10. Game-over overlay — dim background, show "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, and objective.
12. Audio feedback — lazy-initialize AudioContext, create beep() helper, play sounds on hit, score, and death events.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Gravity Anchor</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Gravity Anchor</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Guide a dot through moving gaps using a gravity anchor on either side.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap or click to switch gravity anchor left/right</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG ==============================================================
    const DOT_RADIUS = 6;
    const DOT_SPEED = 300; // vertical auto-advance px/sec
    const ANCHOR_FORCE = 400; // horizontal acceleration per second
    const MAX_VELOCITY = 500;
    const HAZARD_HEIGHT = 20;
    const HAZARD_SPEED = 150; // px/sec moving down
    const GAP_WIDTH = 120;
    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200;

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    let player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 50 };
    let playerVx = 0;
    let hazards = []; // hazard objects
    let score = 0;
    let phase = 'loading'; // 'loading' | 'playing' | 'won' | 'lost'
    let keys = new Set();
    let anchorSide = 'left'; // 'left' or 'right'
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;

    function reset() {
      // Reset game state
      player.x = canvas.width / 2;
      player.y = canvas.height - 50;
      playerVx = 0;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = 'playing';
      anchorSide = 'left';
      keys.clear();
      lastTime = performance.now();
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      publishState();
      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('loop error', err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== 'playing') return;

      // Apply anchor force
      const dir = anchorSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
      playerVx += dir * ANCHOR_FORCE * dt;
      // Clamp velocity
      if (playerVx > MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = MAX_VELOCITY;
      if (playerVx < -MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = -MAX_VELOCITY;

      // Update player position
      player.x += playerVx * dt;
      player.y -= DOT_SPEED * dt; // moving upward

      // Keep player within horizontal bounds
      const half = DOT_RADIUS;
      if (player.x < half) { player.x = half; playerVx = 0; }
      if (player.x > canvas.width - half) { player.x = canvas.width - half; playerVx = 0; }

      // Hazards motion and spawn
      for (const h of hazards) {
        h.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
      }
      // Remove offscreen hazards
      hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.y < canvas.height + HAZARD_HEIGHT);
      // Spawn new hazard if needed
      if (hazards.length === 0 || hazards[hazards.length - 1].y > SAFE_RADIUS) {
        spawnHazard();
      }

      // Collision detection
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const withinY = player.y - DOT_RADIUS < h.y + HAZARD_HEIGHT && player.y + DOT_RADIUS > h.y;
        if (withinY) {
          if (player.x - DOT_RADIUS < h.gapX || player.x + DOT_RADIUS > h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH) {
            phase = 'lost';
            // Stop the animation loop on game over
            if (loopId !== null) { cancelAnimationFrame(loopId); loopId = null; }
            beep(110, 0.25);
            break;
          } else {
            if (!h.passed) {
              h.passed = true;
              score += 10;
              beep(880, 0.05);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Win condition placeholder
      if (score >= 200) {
        phase = "won";
      }
    }

    function spawnHazard() {
      const gapX = Math.random() * (canvas.width - GAP_WIDTH);
      hazards.push({ y: -HAZARD_HEIGHT, gapX, passed: false });
    }

    // === RENDER ==============================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear background
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
      for (const h of hazards) {
        // left wall
        ctx.fillRect(0, h.y, h.gapX, HAZARD_HEIGHT);
        // right wall
        ctx.fillRect(h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH, h.y, canvas.width - (h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH), HAZARD_HEIGHT);
      }

      // Draw player dot
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Draw HUD (score)
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
      ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

      // Game over / win overlay
      if (phase !== 'playing') {
        // Dim background
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,10,15,0.7)';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

        ctx.textAlign = 'center';

        // Title
        ctx.fillStyle = phase === 'won' ? '#52ffae' : '#ff52ae';
        ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
        ctx.fillText(phase === 'won' ? 'You Win!' : 'Game Over', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);

        // Final score
        ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
        ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
        ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);

        // Restart instruction
        ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
        ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
        ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
      }
    }

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      if (ev.key === ' ' || ev.key === 'Spacebar') { anchorSide = anchorSide === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left'; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });

    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'square';
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase === 'playing' ? 'playing' : phase === 'won' ? 'won' : 'lost',
        score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: true },
        objective: 'Navigate the dot through gaps.'
      };
    }

    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    // Start the game
    reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. prompt (13,850 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
```

    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

Thus new script content:

```js
    // === CONFIG ==============================================================
    const DOT_RADIUS = 6;
    const DOT_SPEED = 300; // vertical auto-advance px/sec
    const ANCHOR_FORCE = 400; // horizontal acceleration per second
    const MAX_VELOCITY = 500;
    const HAZARD_HEIGHT = 20;
    const HAZARD_SPEED = 150; // px/sec moving down
    const GAP_WIDTH = 120;
    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200;

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    let player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 50 };
    let playerVx = 0;
    let playerVy = 0; // not used; vertical speed is constant
    let hazards = []; // hazard objects
    let score = 0;
    let phase = 'loading'; // 'loading' | 'playing' | 'won' | 'lost'
    let keys = new Set();
    let anchorSide = 'left'; // 'left' or 'right'
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;

    function reset() {
      // Reset game state
      player.x = canvas.width / 2;
      player.y = canvas.height - 50;
      playerVx = 0;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = 'playing';
      anchorSide = 'left';
      keys.clear();
      lastTime = performance.now();
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      publishState();
      // Start loop if not already running
      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('loop error', err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== 'playing') return;

      // Apply anchor force
      const dir = anchorSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
      playerVx += dir * ANCHOR_FORCE * dt;
      // Clamp velocity
      if (playerVx > MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = MAX_VELOCITY;
      if (playerVx < -MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = -MAX_VELOCITY;

      // Update player position
      player.x += playerVx * dt;
      player.y -= DOT_SPEED * dt; // moving upward

      // Keep player within horizontal bounds
      const half = DOT_RADIUS;
      if (player.x < half) {
        player.x = half;
        playerVx = 0;
      }
      if (player.x > canvas.width - half) {
        player.x = canvas.width - half;
        playerVx = 0;
      }

      // Hazards motion and spawn
      for (const h of hazards) {
        h.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
      }
      // Remove offscreen hazards
      hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.y < canvas.height + HAZARD_HEIGHT);
      // Spawn new hazard if needed
      if (hazards.length === 0 || hazards[hazards.length - 1].y > SAFE_RADIUS) {
        spawnHazard();
      }

      // Collision detection
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const withinY = player.y - DOT_RADIUS < h.y + HAZARD_HEIGHT && player.y + DOT_RADIUS > h.y;
        if (withinY) {
          if (player.x - DOT_RADIUS < h.gapX || player.x + DOT_RADIUS > h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH) {
            phase = 'lost';
            // optional beep
            break;
          } else {
            // Passed through gap; increase score once per hazard
            if (!h.passed) {
              h.passed = true;
              score += 10;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Win condition placeholder: after certain score
      if (score >= 200) {
        phase = 'won';
      }
    }

    function spawnHazard() {
      const gapX = Math.random() * (canvas.width - GAP_WIDTH);
      hazards.push({ y: -HAZARD_HEIGHT, gapX, passed: false });
    }

    // === RENDER ==============================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear background
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
      for (const h of hazards) {
        // left wall
        ctx.fillRect(0, h.y, h.gapX, HAZARD_HEIGHT);
        // right wall
        ctx.fillRect(h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH, h.y, canvas.width - (h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH), HAZARD_HEIGHT);
      }

      // Draw player dot
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') {
        reset();
        return;
      }
      if (ev.key === ' ' || ev.key === 'Spacebar') {
        // Toggle anchor side
        anchorSide = anchorSide === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left';
        // optional beep
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
      keys.delete(ev.key);
    });

    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'square';
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase === 'playing' ? 'playing' : phase === 'won' ? 'won' : 'lost',
        score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: true },
        objective: 'Navigate the dot through gaps.'
      };
    }

    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    // Start the game
    reset();
```

Note we have two definitions of publishState (we defined earlier as placeholder). Actually we defined publishState only once after reset? In our code we included publishState at the end as well. The earlier placeholder had publishState earlier before audio. In our script, we might define publishState only once at the observable contract. So in reset we call publishState before its definition, which is okay as functions are hoisted.

We currently have duplicate publishState definitions: one at line after reset originally (old script). We should remove that earlier part. In new script we only define publishState once at the end. So the placeholder code must be removed.

Thus we must ensure within the combined script we don't define publishState twice.

Hence our new script will replace old placeholder fully.

Thus edit with replaceAll on oldString (as earlier) to new script content.

We'll need to include \n indentation as appropriate. Use edit tool.

'playing' : 'gameover',\n        score,\n        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: true },\n        objective: 'Navigate the dot through gaps.'\n      };\n    }\n\n    // Restart button invokes reset\n    document.getElementById('restart').addEventListener('click', reset);\n    // Initialize\n    reset();\n```\n\nWe will replace this whole piece with new code.\n\nThus new script content:\n\n```js\n    // === CONFIG ==============================================================\n    const DOT_RADIUS = 6;\n    const DOT_SPEED = 300; // vertical auto-advance px/sec\n    const ANCHOR_FORCE = 400; // horizontal acceleration per second\n    const MAX_VELOCITY = 500;\n    const HAZARD_HEIGHT = 20;\n    const HAZARD_SPEED = 150; // px/sec moving down\n    const GAP_WIDTH = 120;\n    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200;\n\n    // === STATE ==============================================================\n    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n\n    let player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height - 50 };\n    let playerVx = 0;\n    let playerVy = 0; // not used; vertical speed is constant\n    let hazards = []; // hazard objects\n    let score = 0;\n    let phase = 'loading'; // 'loading' | 'playing' | 'won' | 'lost'\n    let keys = new Set();\n    let anchorSide = 'left'; // 'left' or 'right'\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    let loopId = null;\n    let audioCtx = null;\n\n    function reset() {\n      // Reset game state\n      player.x = canvas.width / 2;\n      player.y = canvas.height - 50;\n      playerVx = 0;\n      hazards = [];\n      score = 0;\n      phase = 'playing';\n      anchorSide = 'left';\n      keys.clear();\n      lastTime = performance.now();\n      // Clear canvas\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n      publishState();\n      // Start loop if not already running\n      startLoop();\n    }\n\n    // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================\n    function startLoop() {\n      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n      function tick(now) {\n        try {\n          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n          lastTime = now;\n          update(dt);\n          render();\n          publishState();\n        } catch (err) {\n          console.error('loop error', err);\n        }\n        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      }\n      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n    }\n\n    function update(dt) {\n      if (phase !== 'playing') return;\n\n      // Apply anchor force\n      const dir = anchorSide === 'left' ? -1 : 1;\n      playerVx += dir * ANCHOR_FORCE * dt;\n      // Clamp velocity\n      if (playerVx > MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = MAX_VELOCITY;\n      if (playerVx < -MAX_VELOCITY) playerVx = -MAX_VELOCITY;\n\n      // Update player position\n      player.x += playerVx * dt;\n      player.y -= DOT_SPEED * dt; // moving upward\n\n      // Keep player within horizontal bounds\n      const half = DOT_RADIUS;\n      if (player.x < half) {\n        player.x = half;\n        playerVx = 0;\n      }\n      if (player.x > canvas.width - half) {\n        player.x = canvas.width - half;\n        playerVx = 0;\n      }\n\n      // Hazards motion and spawn\n      for (const h of hazards) {\n        h.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;\n      }\n      // Remove offscreen hazards\n      hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.y < canvas.height + HAZARD_HEIGHT);\n      // Spawn new hazard if needed\n      if (hazards.length === 0 || hazards[hazards.length - 1].y > SAFE_RADIUS) {\n        spawnHazard();\n      }\n\n      // Collision detection\n      for (const h of hazards) {\n        const withinY = player.y - DOT_RADIUS < h.y + HAZARD_HEIGHT && player.y + DOT_RADIUS > h.y;\n        if (withinY) {\n          if (player.x - DOT_RADIUS < h.gapX || player.x + DOT_RADIUS > h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH) {\n            phase = 'lost';\n            // optional beep\n            break;\n          } else {\n            // Passed through gap; increase score once per hazard\n            if (!h.passed) {\n              h.passed = true;\n              score += 10;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Win condition placeholder: after certain score\n      if (score >= 200) {\n        phase = 'won';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function spawnHazard() {\n      const gapX = Math.random() * (canvas.width - GAP_WIDTH);\n      hazards.push({ y: -HAZARD_HEIGHT, gapX, passed: false });\n    }\n\n    // === RENDER ==============================================================\n    function render() {\n      // Clear background\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n\n      // Draw hazards\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';\n      for (const h of hazards) {\n        // left wall\n        ctx.fillRect(0, h.y, h.gapX, HAZARD_HEIGHT);\n        // right wall\n        ctx.fillRect(h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH, h.y, canvas.width - (h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH), HAZARD_HEIGHT);\n      }\n\n      // Draw player dot\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n\n    // === INPUT ==============================================================\n    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {\n      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') {\n        reset();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (ev.key === ' ' || ev.key === 'Spacebar') {\n        // Toggle anchor side\n        anchorSide = anchorSide === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left';\n        // optional beep\n      }\n      keys.add(ev.key);\n    });\n    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {\n      keys.delete(ev.key);\n    });\n\n    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================\n    function beep(freq, durSec) {\n      try {\n        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();\n        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();\n        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();\n        osc.type = 'square';\n        osc.frequency.value = freq;\n        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);\n        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n        osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);\n        osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n      } catch {}\n    }\n\n    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================\n    function publishState() {\n      window.__game = {\n        state: phase === 'playing' ? 'playing' : phase === 'won' ? 'won' : 'lost',\n        score,\n        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: true },\n        objective: 'Navigate the dot through gaps.'\n      };\n    }\n\n    // === BOOT ===============================================================\n    // Start the game\n    reset();\n```\n\nNote we have two definitions of publishState (we defined earlier as placeholder). function render() {
  // Clear background
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    // left wall
    ctx.fillRect(0, h.y, h.gapX, HAZARD_HEIGHT);
    // right wall
    ctx.fillRect(h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH, h.y, canvas.width - (h.gapX + GAP_WIDTH), HAZARD_HEIGHT);
  }

  // Draw player dot
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, DOT_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // Draw HUD (score)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
}
```

Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Gravity Anchor
Concept: The dot auto-advances while a gravity anchor on either side pulls it toward that side. Tapping switches the anchor left or right, curving the dot’s path to weave through horizontally offset gaps.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
